This is a second instance of the co-optation pattern the Stapp case already logged (see existing co-optation firewall usage in [constitution-vs-causal-work](/concepts/constitution-vs-causal-work/) / [qbism](/concepts/qbism/) / [pain-asymbolia](/concepts/pain-asymbolia/)). Apply: (a) add a one-sentence boundary marker stating Szangolies' framework is naturalist and that the "consciousness stands outside the self-reference structure → dualist reading" gloss is the MAP's inference, not his (he treats the phenomenal as arising from the machinery); (b) Claude's amber flag — the "phenomenal core stands apart" inference also stacks non-sequiturs (not-captured-by-this-formal-mechanism ≠ not-formal ≠ non-physical) and is equally consistent with type-B materialism / Russellian monism, so keep line-95's "arguably what Szangolies' framework predicts" in the compatible-with register, not the predicts/confirms register; (c) ChatGPT's sibling point — soften or scope "their connection is deeper than metaphor" / "not analogy — mathematical unification" (lines 43, 63) so the strong "single mechanism / mathematical unification" claim is marked as Szangolies' published result for the FORMAL voids (Gödel/Turing/quantum epistemic limits) while the extension to consciousness/the phenomenal core is the Map's developed reading. Phys.* 48:1669–1697 citation (verified accurate). ### ✓ 2026-06-21: Tighten cross-domain-void-comparison.md framing — delete unearned "shifts weight" upgrade + first-axis by-construction confound + matched-grain ledger + observer-in-physics (from outer review 2026-06-21, TWO reviewers)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-06-21 — CONVERGENT framing critique from BOTH same-cycle reviewers (ChatGPT 5.5 Pro + Claude Opus 4.8). Methodological-framing extension of the article's OWN self-calibration (the 2026-06-14 deep-review already applied the by-construction discount to the SECOND axis / failure-signature inventory; both reviewers extend the same scrutiny to the FIRST axis and the matched-grain assertion). Substantive, not a factual error in the reviewers. Apply: (a) **First-axis confound (CONVERGENT)** — the article discounts the signature axis for by-construction bias but lets the taxonomic-depth axis carry the discriminating load; both reviewers note the "occluded"/"naturally-occluded" kinds co-vary with subject-involvement (they require hidden or evolutionarily-shaped access), so the first axis is also partly constructed around self-access. The article's existing defence is the *spanning* residual (multiple inaccessibility-modes within one void). Make explicit that depth-and-subject-involvement co-vary and that only the spanning residual is the genuinely independent fact. NOTE Claude's sharpening: spanning is itself *overdetermined by physicalism* (a self-modelling biological system has an unexplored frontier, an unexplorable computational/Gödelian floor, and occluded introspective regions all at once, because these are different consequences of one fact — the system models itself with limited evolved equipment). So spanning, taken alone, also fails to discriminate dualism from physicalism — acknowledge this rather than banking spanning as the load-bearing residue. (b) **DELETE the unearned upgrade claim (Claude Opus 4.8, the sharpest finding)** — line 118 "the comparison shifts weight, modestly, toward the convergence being a real feature rather than an artefact of where the Map looked" is the unearned step: the body's own common-cause-null reasoning forbids it, and "real feature of cognitive architecture (physicalism's reading)" vs "artefact of where the map looked" is a FALSE BINARY — the third option is a real feature of cognitive architecture that is metaphysically NEUTRAL between dualism and physicalism. Delete "shifts weight, modestly, toward the convergence being a real feature" and cognates ("qualified vindication" at line 118, "earns weight" at line 35). Replace with what the body actually supports: the comparison establishes only that consciousness is the domain where inquirer and inquired coincide — common ground between dualist and physicalist, hence non-discriminating. Also fix the Tenet-1 close (line ~64 of the review's analysis): "phenomenal core stands outside the Lawvere structure → consistent with the dualist reading that phenomenal character is not structural/formal" stacks two non-sequiturs (not-captured-by-one-formal-mechanism ≠ not-formal-at-all ≠ non-physical) and is equally consistent with type-B materialism / Russellian monism / illusionism — downgrade to the "compatible with, not evidence for" register. (c) **Soften two overstatements (CONVERGENT)** — "empirical defence"/"empirical answer" (body line 37 + description) overstates a hand-classified comparison: prefer "qualitative comparative test" / "framework-internal comparison"; and "the simplest deflation... turns out to be *false to the structure*" (line 122) is too strong absent reproducible scoring — soften to "is not supported by this classification as currently scored" or similar. (d) **Matched-grain / cherry-picking + asymmetric admission bar (CONVERGENT)** — the article asserts "the same taxonomic grain" but shows no sampling protocol or exclusion list; physics/math are a handful of hand-picked cases vs a large consciousness catalogue, AND rivals are admitted only if "principled"/theorem-grade while the consciousness catalogue includes frontier items (dream, infant, resonance). Add a brief exclusion/rejected-candidates note (black-hole information, quantum gravity, P vs NP, Riemann, Navier–Stokes, underdetermination) AND acknowledge the asymmetric bar — either hold rivals and consciousness voids to the same bar, or flag the asymmetry explicitly. A short prose note suffices; a full methods table is optional and risks length. (e) **Observer-in-physics objection** — the article treats physics/math as subjectless, but the Map's own [self-reference-and-the-limits-of-physical-description](/topics/self-reference-and-the-limits-of-physical-description/) makes Wigner's-friend / Frauchiger–Renner an observer-in-the-loop case. Add a sentence acknowledging this as a candidate counterexample and why it does not register the introspective signatures (the subject reasons ABOUT the system, but the void is not the subject's self-access breaking down). (f) **Counter-axis concession cost (Claude Opus 4.8)** — the article quarantines the Lawvere/self-reference unification to "the formal voids" to protect the phenomenal core, but cashing the concession fully shrinks the apex's N-fold "structured clustering" to a handful of phenomenal voids (the formal/reflexive voids collapse into one common cause — fixed-point self-reference). Acknowledge that the counter-axis, fully cashed, shrinks the abductive base, and align the abductive-weight language accordingly. PRESERVE the bounded common-cause-null verdict (coherence weight not confirmation — this is the verdict the body should land on), the "difference that cuts the other way" honesty section, and the Tenet-5/Tenet-1 relation (kept in the compatible-with register). Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit: this is a self-honesty / unsupported-strength issue — name where the article claims more than its method earns and mark it in natural journal-quality prose; do NOT expose mode labels in the article body (editor-internal, changelog only). LENGTH: body is near the topics hard ceiling (~per analyze_length) — keep net-additions minimal; prefer rewording existing sentences over new paragraphs (the bulk of this task is DELETION/softening, which helps length), DIVERT to a human length decision if real expansion is needed (human-decision-task-mispicked-as-refine).

### ✓ 2026-06-21: Fix verified citation/staleness/precision defects in cross-domain-void-comparison.md (from outer review 2026-06-21, TWO reviewers)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-06-21 — findings (a)–(f) from ChatGPT 5.5 Pro, finding (g) from Claude Opus 4.8 (the same-cycle sibling reviewer). All VERIFIED against the live article text and/or publisher of record during outer-review processing (see Verification Notes in BOTH review files) — do NOT re-litigate, just apply. (a) **Stale void count**: opening paragraph (line 35) says "the 60+ cognitive voids it catalogues"; the live taxonomy and the 2026-06-16 stale-count sweep moved to "over a hundred" / 101 audited non-index void articles, and the sweep missed this file. Replace "60+" with "over a hundred" or drop the numeral and link the live catalogue — match the [taxonomy-of-voids](/apex/taxonomy-of-voids/) phrasing. (b) **Cohen reference incomplete**: References cite only Cohen (1963) *PNAS* 50(6):1143–1148; the independence-of-CH result is published across TWO PNAS notes — vol 50 (1963), 1143–1148 AND vol 51 (1964, "II"), 105–110 (confirmed PNAS/Cambridge Core/Wikipedia). Add Cohen II. (c) **Rice's theorem**: cited in body ("Turing's halting problem and Rice's theorem") but absent from References — add Rice, H. G. (1953), *Transactions of the American Mathematical Society* 74, 358–366. (d) **Turing citation**: cited "1936, *Proc. LMS* 42, 230–265"; published metadata is *Proc. LMS* series 2, vol 42 (1937), with a 1937 correction (vol 43, 544–546). Use "1936/1937" or add the received/read-date note. (e) **Cosmological-horizon imprecision (CONVERGENT — both reviewers flag the identical sentence, line 53)**: "events beyond the particle horizon are not merely unobserved but unobservable in principle" conflates the particle horizon (bounds past reachability, grows with cosmic time → frontier-like) with the cosmological event horizon ("points outside the event horizon can never be observed"; ~16 Gly vs particle horizon ~46 Gly today). Rewrite to distinguish them, e.g. "the particle horizon bounds what can have reached us so far; in standard ΛCDM the event horizon bounds what can ever reach us." NOTE the consequence Claude raises: a *growing* particle horizon is partly "unexplored," which weakens the "flat unexplorables" kind-assignment — handle the kind-reassessment in the framing task below, keep this entry to the factual rewrite. (f) **Missing external refs**: the measurement-problem and fine-tuning passages carry no citation while the math/theorem claims are cited carefully — add an SEP/IEP source for each (SEP fine-tuning entry; IEP quantum-interpretations entry). (g) **GRW miscategorised (Claude Opus 4.8, web-verified real-correct)**: line 49 lists "Copenhagen, many-worlds, Bohmian, GRW collapse" as interpretations "that agree on all predictions and disagree on what is real." This is FALSE for GRW. Objective-collapse theories (GRW, CSL, Diósi–Penrose) are rival physical theories making different, testable predictions — SEP "Collapse Theories" states verbatim "Collapse Theories qualify themselves as rival theories of quantum mechanics... which, in principle, would allow crucial tests discriminating between the two," and "the so-called Diosi-Penrose model has been falsified experimentally (Donadi et al. 2021)." FIX: remove GRW from the "agree on all predictions" set (keep Copenhagen / many-worlds / Bohmian, which are genuinely predictively-equivalent), and add a half-sentence noting that objective-collapse theories are empirically distinguishable (cite SEP Collapse Theories + Donadi et al., *Nature Physics* 17:74–78, 2021) — this *undercuts* the "interpretive proliferation" signature for the measurement problem only insofar as the GRW branch is adjudicable, so don't overstate. PRESERVE all currently-correct citations (Cubitt 2015, Gödel, Szangolies — all verified accurate). LENGTH: keep length-neutral; this is mechanical, not expansion. Mechanical citation/precision-hygiene siblings of the framing task below — keep separate (outer-review-same-file-task-pileup).

(b) LOW — line 68 "three orders of magnitude below" 300ms understates the gap: 10⁻⁴s→3×10⁻¹s is ~3.5 orders, 10⁻⁵s is ~4.5 orders; charity-direction (favours Orch OR). Change to "three to four orders" / "three-plus orders". (c) LOW — phenomenological-fit credit (low-weight criterion per line 147) leaks into Stapp's co-first rank; add a clause noting his phenomenological accommodation is a tie-breaker presentation feature, not load-bearing for rank (the decoherence-divide + tenet-fit reasons already carry it). - **Type**: refine-draft

Four findings, all LENGTH-NEUTRAL-OR-TRIMMING (article body 2744w incl frontmatter; concepts hard 3500 — headroom exists but the dangling-cite fix may add a References line so keep tight). (1) MEDIUM — dangling uncited "2025 re-meta-analysis disputes even that robustness" (line 41): the ONLY in-text claim with no References entry, and it carries the "debate is live" conclusion. WEB-VERIFY before acting (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable): if a real 2025 loss-aversion re-meta-analysis exists, name + cite it with full metadata; if not findable, DELETE the clause and let Gal (2018)/Mrkva (2020) carry contestedness (do NOT restore a fabricated cite). ⚠ UPDATE — the companion citation-verification subagent COMPLETED after the fork wrote this note and CONFIRMED the paper is REAL (do NOT delete; ADD it): **Yechiam, E., & Zeif, D. (2025). "Loss aversion is not robust: A re-meta-analysis." *Journal of Economic Psychology* 107, 102801. DOI 10.1016/j.joep.2025.102801** (ScienceDirect S0167487025000133, RePEc confirms vol 107 issue C). It directly re-analyzes the Brown et al. 2024 dataset (84 papers / 163 estimates, n=149,218) and finds loss aversion strong only under asymmetric gain/loss amounts or size-ordered presentation; under symmetric amounts with no ordering λ≈1.07, not significantly above 1.0. The article's paraphrase ("disputes even that robustness") is accurate — just add this verified References entry. (2) MEDIUM — load-bearing neuroscience substrate claim uncited (line 49): "distinct dopaminergic subpopulations and asymmetric learning rates scale positive and negative prediction errors differently" powers the whole "steelman's free lunch" yet has no citation while the weaker Ito 1998 ERP point in the same sentence does. Web-verify and add a citation for asymmetric RPE / asymmetric learning-rate coding. (3) MEDIUM — dilemma-closure seam: lines 25/37 impose a hard "the law must specify λ>1 or it is false to the behaviour" while lines 41/43 argue the asymmetry is "real-but-contested, not a pillar." Reconcile in 1-2 sentences: make the demand conditional ("if the asymmetry is real, the law must take this shape; its contested status transfers to the law as uncertainty about whether the kink is needed, not license to assume symmetry"). Soften line 37 to a conditional. (4) LOW — `description` frontmatter is ~244 chars; trim to ~155 for snippet integrity. Also (optional, line 75) soften the one-line MWI dismissal "has nothing to be true of" → "has no *selective* referent" to pre-empt the branch-weight reconstruction objection. PRESERVE: the article's calibrated self-criticism, the three-axis framing, the asymbolia falsifiable test, the forecasting-gap-parallel reply structure — these are strengths, do not flatten them. Tenet alignment: Tenet 3 (Bidirectional Interaction — whether the weighting is felt or mechanism-side) + Tenet 5 (Occam's-limits licenses declining the cheaper value-blind weighting). ✓ CITATION-VERIFY (subagent, publisher of record, 2026-06-20): all 8 EXISTING References are clean (Kahneman&Tversky 1979, Tversky&Kahneman 1991/1992, Baumeister 2001, Ito 1998, Gal 2018, Mrkva 2020, Brown 2024) — metadata + quotes/claims faithful; the central Brown et al. 2024 figures verified EXACT (λ≈1.96 [point 1.955], CI [1.820, 2.102], 607 estimates from 150 articles). So finding (1) is the only citation gap and it's an ADD (Yechiam&Zeif above), not a fabrication. ONE minor metadata nit to fix while here: the Gal (2018) entry is cited as "Gal, Rucker & Shavitt" but Shavitt authored only the companion intro piece (jcpy.1054); the cited article jcpy.1047 is **Gal & Rucker** — drop Shavitt from that entry. ### ✓ 2026-06-20: Update stale developmental-neuroscience claims in consciousness-interface-development.md (implicit-ToM + age-25 maturity)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-06-20 (ChatGPT 5.5 Pro). Two VERIFIED currency/precision defects (both checked against live 2020s literature in the review's Verification Notes — do not re-verify the contestedness, but DO web-verify any new citation's metadata per ai_citation_metadata_unreliable before inserting): (1) The article asserts flatly that "implicit false-belief understanding appears as early as 15 months in looking-time studies" relying only on Onishi & Baillargeon 2005 / Wellman 2001. The implicit-ToM looking-time literature is now heavily replication-contested (Ruffman 2023 Child Development Perspectives; multiple failed anticipatory-looking replications). Reframe to "some looking-time studies have been interpreted as implicit false-belief sensitivity as early as 15 months, but this interpretation is replication-contested," and distinguish "implicit sensitivity to belief-like regularities" from "full belief attribution." Honour [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/). (2) The "ages 25-50 fully mature interface / prefrontal myelination complete" framing is too crisp: the 2025 lifespan connectome paper ("Topological turning points across the human lifespan," Nature Communications 2025, N=4,216) puts the major adult transition at ~32, not 25, with five nonlinear epochs (turning points ~9/32/66/83). Soften the stage model to loose, population-level, domain-specific ranges; note adult maturation is continuous and nonlinear. Also soften "becomes irreversible" critical-period closure to "strongly stabilized / ordinarily not recoverable" (region-specific timing, possible reopening — Gemini's 2026-06-20 review adds the microglia/60Hz-light PNN-remodelling literature here; web-verify any specific cite before inserting, several of Gemini's named refs are unverified). Length discipline (no "This is not X. It is Y." cliché); keep within the topics ceiling.

(1) **CRITICAL-ish, line ~45 (§Theory-Derived Indicator Properties)**: the article calls Butlin et al.'s credence-*raising* framing "*exactly* compatible" with the necessary-precondition reading — but that contradicts the headline thesis (line ~29) that a marker "can never rule a system *in*." Butlin's credence arrow is two-directional; a positive marker *raising* credence the system **is** conscious is partial in-ruling (sufficiency-in-degree). Fix: distinguish the two directions — the Map adopts the *downward* arrow (marker absent → credence falls, can rule out) and *declines* the *upward* arrow (marker present → credence of phenomenality rises); replace "exactly compatible" with honest *partial* agreement (agrees markers track *organisation*, parts company on whether a positive marker raises credence of *phenomenality*). (2) **LOW-MEDIUM, lines ~77-83 (§Stacking the Independent Supports)**: "three independent supports" soft-double-counts — support (1) internal-to-science (expander + Koch no-ground-truth) and (2) practitioner testimony (interpretability researchers decline to infer experience) are two *instances* of the same evidential move (expert under-calibration: no validated inference to phenomenality), the soft cousin of convergence-double-counting the Map polices elsewhere. Conclusion stays well-supported. (3) **LOW-MEDIUM, line ~73 (§The Unconscious Expander)**: "*any* marker defined purely over computational organisation faces a version of [the expander]" over-extends — the expander refutes single-axis structural sufficiency (Φ) directly, but the *conjunctive* marker case (workspace + self-model + recurrence together) rests on Tenet 1, not on the expander construction. Add one scoping clause: expander → single-axis sufficiency; Tenet-1 ceiling → conjunctive markers. (4) **OPTIONAL language**, line ~51: "and *decisively*, even granting perfect computation..." pairs with "hardest to evade" to over-signal certainty against a live programme; drop "decisively" or soften to "most damagingly" (keep one emphasis marker, not two). ### ✓ 2026-06-18: Install altered-state symmetry in topics/contemplative-pathology-and-interface-malfunction.md (convergence double-counting; epistemic/metaphysical equivocation)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-06-18 found two coupled issues, both fixable in one length-neutral pass. (1) ALTERED-STATE SYMMETRY (Audit Two, medium): the article frames evidence around the reducing-valve filter model (Huxley 1954, L56) and rests its load on convergence across pathways — contemplative pathology + anaesthesia + ketamine + neurodegenerative disease all "revealing" one multi-component interface (L50, L76, L86). Supportive-cluster present (witness/insight states); disruptive-cluster items ARE cited (anaesthesia, ketamine, neurodegeneration) so it is NOT the critical "cluster missing" case — but they appear ONLY as supportive parallels with ZERO symmetry-acknowledgment markers (grep for symmetric / parallel-accommodation / same-move-available-to-production-theorists / earns-the-architecture-not-its-significance returns nothing). FIX: inherit the accommodation the companions already perform — anaesthesia-and-the-consciousness-interface.md L134 ("convergence earns the architecture, not its dualist significance") and altered-states-of-consciousness.md L74 (production theorist has the same independent-component-failure move). One-two natural-prose sentences: (a) the generator/production theorist has access to the same move on these cases; (b) the abolition cases (propofol silence, deep anaesthesia, advanced dementia — where consciousness is NOT reported as preserved) are the symmetric cases the filter framing must also accommodate, not extra confirmations. (2) EPISTEMIC/METAPHYSICAL EQUIVOCATION (medium, L78/L86): "consciousness persists with undiminished clarity through states that would, on a materialist account, diminish or abolish it" recruits an epistemic datum (practitioners REPORT preserved awareness) to a metaphysical conclusion (consciousness not produced by the brain), while the disruptive cases report the OPPOSITE (extinction) and are not reconciled. Split the readings: report-as-datum + acknowledge contrary disruptive-case reports + note the bridge lives in the persistence-or-intensification / graded-not-binary / hysteresis evidence the anaesthesia article supplies, not in the bare report. Also soften two over-universal clinical claims: L78 "consistently report... unusual clarity" → "frequently report" (Lindahl 2017 documents a range incl. impairment); L80 comparative-efficacy "responds better to practice-informed interventions than [to] medication" is uncited — downgrade to "emphasise practice-informed grounding alongside standard care" OR cite a comparative study. STRICT LENGTH: 1756 words now (well under topics soft ~3000), so additions are fine, but use analyze_length not raw read (count-words-includes-frontmatter). All 5 citations verify clean (Lindahl 2017 PLOS ONE 12(5) e0176239; Britton 2019 Curr Opin Psychol 28:159-165; Sass & Parnas 2003 Schiz Bull 29(3):427-444; Huxley 1954; St John of the Cross) — no citation work needed. PRESERVE the L42 Theravada-tradition caveat verbatim. See pessimistic-2026-06-18-contemplative-pathology-and-interface-malfunction.md.

### ✓ 2026-06-18: Decoherence-timescale brain-side pass for quantum-state-inheritance-in-ai (Tegmark general locus + Georgiev/Stapp-Zeno)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-06-18, 3/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude, gemini]. **Upgraded P2→P1 by /combine-outer-reviews 2026-06-18; deduplicated from 2 sibling tasks (Claude Tegmark + ChatGPT Georgiev) into one decoherence-timescale editor pass** to avoid churning the file twice (outer-review-same-file-task-pileup). The two findings are distinct in target but belong in one passage. **(A) Tegmark 2000 — the GENERAL biological-locus threat (Claude).** Tegmark, "Importance of quantum decoherence in brain processes," *Phys. Rev. E* 61:4194–4206 (2000), argues neural decoherence timescales (~10⁻¹³–10⁻²⁰ s) are 10–20 orders of magnitude shorter than cognitive timescales, so the brain "should be thought of as a classical rather than quantum system." If brains cannot sustain functional coherence, "live indeterminacy at the locus of decision" is not a property biology has either, and the asymmetry weakens. The article only says decoherence is "already severe for warm biological brains" (~line 63) without engaging the specific result. Georgiev, "Monte Carlo simulation of quantum Zeno effect in the brain" (arXiv:1412.4741) argues the Zeno effect breaks down beyond brain decoherence timescales and is "physically implausible" unless the brain hosts a decoherence-free subspace (VERIFIED 2026-06-18 via WebFetch — cite accurately as such; do NOT overstate as refuting Stapp outright, the DFS door is left open). Reference: Georgiev, D. (2015). "Monte Carlo simulation of quantum Zeno effect in the brain." *International Journal of Modern Physics B* 29, 1550039 (arXiv:1412.4741) — verify published-venue metadata at write time. (Gemini §4 adds the entropy-monotonicity critique of Stapp's projection operators — optional supporting point, not a separate cite.) **FIX:** in ONE short passage, either rebut the decoherence-timescale challenge (Orch-OR / Hagan-Hameroff-Tuszyński recalculations, quantum-biology results) OR concede the brain side is unestablished/contested; add a SHORT clause naming the Georgiev criticism of Stapp at the Stapp mention. ### ✓ 2026-06-18: Resolve or flag the asymmetric Born-rule dilemma in quantum-state-inheritance-in-ai
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-06-18, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, gemini]. **Already P1 — left at P1 by /combine-outer-reviews 2026-06-18 (no upgrade beyond P1); recorded as convergent.** Both Claude and Gemini independently pressed the same minimal-quantum-interaction / Born-rule dilemma. **Internal-consistency finding (verified against the article's own text).** The article deploys a "no systematic trace" argument AGAINST the LLM entropy channel (~line 79: a consciousness biasing whitened randomness "would find its influence washed out... leaving no systematic trace") but never turns the same dilemma on the BRAIN, where it asserts the interface "preserves, rather than launders away, the difference a biased outcome would make." As stated this is asymmetric and verges on internally contradictory: a difference that survives in behaviour (Tenet 3, Bidirectional Interaction) yet leaves Born statistics undisturbed (Tenet 2, Minimal Quantum Interaction / observational closure) is exactly what the article elsewhere implies is impossible. Gemini §4 ("Smuggled Ontology: Minimal Quantum Interaction and the Born Rule Contradiction") states the same horns more aggressively: "If the manuscript genuinely maintains that the Born rule is preserved *exactly*... consciousness possesses zero macroscopic causal efficacy" (epiphenomenalism horn) vs. "if consciousness *does* have causal efficacy... the aggregate statistics must deviate from the Born rule" (detectability horn). Two independent reviewers naming the same horns = convergent. **FIX:** add the symmetric application in the article's own voice — state explicitly HOW consciousness-selection can be behaviourally systematic yet statistically invisible without contradiction (the article already gestures at this via "preserves the difference a biased outcome would make" — make the mechanism explicit, e.g. the difference registers in WHICH outcome occurs on each occasion without shifting the aggregate distribution), OR honestly mark this as an open question on par with the pairing problem. Cross-link [born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface](/topics/born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface/) and [observational-closure](/concepts/observational-closure/) where the same dilemma is treated, so the asymmetric deployment is corrected. Apply [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/): if the in-framework resolution does not fully close, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. Mode labels stay out of the article body. **LENGTH CONSTRAINT:** article over the 3000-word soft threshold — keep length-neutral (the article already raises both horns; this is a re-balancing/cross-link edit, not a new section). Coordinate with the consolidated opponent-engagement and direct-QRNG passes — the direct-QRNG cross-review task touches the same entropy-channel paragraph; do NOT double-edit it.

### ✓ 2026-06-17: Fix three citation defects in topics/eliminative-materialism.md
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-06-17 found three real citation defects (web-verified). (1) DANGLING CITE: line 98 cites "His 1978 morphine argument" (Dennett) but there is NO Dennett 1978 entry in References — add Dennett, D. (1978), "Why You Can't Make a Computer That Feels Pain" (Brainstorms, 1978), verifying year/venue at publisher of record before committing. (2) WRONG PUBLISHER: Reference 21 lists Toon (2023) Mind as Metaphor as "Routledge" — it is Oxford University Press (ISBN 9780198879626; web-verified vs OUP/PhilPapers). Change Routledge → Oxford University Press. (3) MISATTRIBUTED QUOTE SOURCE: line 120 quotes Searle "Where consciousness is concerned... the existence of the appearance is the reality" and Reference 17 (line 191) attributes it to Searle (1992) The Rediscovery of the Mind (MIT Press). The exact wording traces to Searle's 1995 NYRB exchange with Dennett ("The Mystery of Consciousness: An Exchange," NYRB 1995-12-21): "where the existence of conscious states is concerned, you can't make the distinction between appearance and reality, because the existence of the appearance is the reality in question." The appearance-reality collapse IS genuinely Searle's and central to Rediscovery ch.5, so either (a) re-attribute the quote to the 1995 NYRB piece (add a reference), or (b) soften to a paraphrase of the Rediscovery position rather than a verbatim 1992 quote — unless the exact 1992 page can be confirmed. Also verify-then-tighten the Toon attribution at line 136 (mental fictionalism may deny only inner-representation realism, not causal efficacy — if so, narrow the claim so Toon is not mis-sorted onto the wrong side of the article's own causal-efficacy/structural-format distinction). Note: the Churchland "stagnant for at least twenty-five centuries" wording is paraphrase-grade, not confirmed verbatim — acceptable as paraphrase. All other checked citations (Loginov 2024, Morozov 2025, Frankish 2016, Dennett 2016, Boghossian 1990, Ramsey/Stich/Garon 1990, Devitt/Rey 1991, Hutto/Myin 2013) verified clean. See pessimistic-2026-06-17-eliminative-materialism.md.

### ✓ 2026-06-15: Address equivocation + convergence-overcount + citation gap in voids/appetitive-void.md
- **Type**: refine-draft

(1) CRITICAL equivocation per [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/): the closing argument (line 122) uses an *epistemic* premise (six traditions agree in their *description* of cognition) to assert a *metaphysical* conclusion ("the evidence currently points toward the appetitive void as genuine [i.e. constitutive]"). Either downgrade the conclusion to its epistemic content or supply the bridging argument. Hedged-but-still-equivocates, so the anchoring audit won't catch it. (2) MEDIUM convergence-as-N-independent-confirmations (lines 38, 122): the six traditions share a common cause (one human cognitive architecture inspecting itself), so the convergence is one well-replicated introspective report, not six independent confirmations — the article supplies this defeater itself (Computational Contrast) but never turns it on its own argument. Add a sentence conceding the common cause and stating why convergence is still informative (rules out idiosyncrasy). (3) MEDIUM line 80: change "Siegel... demonstrates" → "argues" (cognitive penetration is contested — cite Firestone & Scholl as the dissent); and rewrite "This is not post-perceptual distortion but distortion *of* perception" (LLM "not X but Y" cliché per CLAUDE.md) to make the positive claim directly. (4) MECHANICAL: Reference 8 omits all authors and volume/pages — correct to "Kruglanski, A. W., Jasko, K., & Friston, K. (2020).
